#include <chrono>
#include <cmath>
#include <cstdio>
#include <cstdlib>
#include <cstring>
#include <vector>

#include "../common/results_output.hpp"

// Generate pseudo-random values for matrix initialization
constexpr double getPseudoRndValue(const size_t N, const size_t i, const size_t j) noexcept {
    return (((i + 1) * (i + j + 1) * 1299709) % (N * N)) / static_cast<double>(N * N);
}

void initMatrix(std::vector<double>& mat, const size_t N) {
    for (size_t i = 0; i < N; ++i) {
        for (size_t j = 0; j < N; ++j) {
            mat[i * N + j] = getPseudoRndValue(N, i, j);
        }
    }
}

void matrixMultiply(const std::vector<double>& A, const std::vector<double>& B, 
                    std::vector<double>& C, const size_t N) {
    for (size_t i = 0; i < N; ++i) {
        for (size_t j = 0; j < N; ++j) {
            double sum = 0.0;
            for (size_t k = 0; k < N; ++k) {
                sum += A[i * N + k] * B[k * N + j];
            }
            C[i * N + j] = sum;
        }
    }
}

// Simple validation: compute a single element and compare
bool validateResult(const std::vector<double>& A, const std::vector<double>& B,
                   const std::vector<double>& C, const size_t N) {
    // Check a few random positions
    constexpr size_t checkPoints[] = {0, 1, 2, 3, 4};
    
    for (size_t pi = 0; pi < 5; ++pi) {
        for (size_t pj = 0; pj < 5; ++pj) {
            const size_t i = checkPoints[pi] % N;
            const size_t j = checkPoints[pj] % N;
            
            double expected = 0.0;
            for (size_t k = 0; k < N; ++k) {
                expected += A[i * N + k] * B[k * N + j];
            }
            
            const double actual = C[i * N + j];
            const double relError = std::abs((actual - expected) / (expected + 1e-10));
            
            if (relError > 1e-6) {
                printf("Validation failed at (%zu, %zu): expected %.10f, got %.10f (error: %.10e)\n",
                       i, j, expected, actual, relError);
                return false;
            }
        }
    }
    
    return true;
}

void printUsage(const char* progName) {
    printf("Usage: %s [options]\n", progName);
    printf("Options:\n");
    printf("  -n <num>     Matrix size N (computes NxN * NxN) (default: 512)\n");
    printf("  -v           Enable validation\n");
    printf("  -r           Print results for external validation\n");
    printf("  -h           Show this help message\n");
}

int main(int argc, char** argv) {
    size_t N = 512;
    bool validate = false;
    bool printResults = false;
    
    // Parse command line arguments
    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {
        if (strcmp(argv[i], "-n") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            N = atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-v") == 0) {
            validate = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-r") == 0) {
            printResults = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-h") == 0) {
            printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 0;
        } else {
            printf("Unknown option: %s\n", argv[i]);
            printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 1;
        }
    }
    
    printf("Matrix Multiplication Benchmark\n");
    printf("Matrix size: %zu x %zu\n", N, N);
    printf("Validation: %s\n", validate ? "enabled" : "disabled");
    
    // Allocate matrices
    std::vector<double> A(N * N);
    std::vector<double> B(N * N);
    std::vector<double> C(N * N);
    
    // Initialize matrices
    printf("Initializing matrices...\n");
    initMatrix(A, N);
    initMatrix(B, N);
    
    // Perform matrix multiplication
    printf("Computing matrix multiplication...\n");
    auto start = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
    
    matrixMultiply(A, B, C, N);
    
    auto end = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
    auto duration = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(end - start);
    
    printf("Computation time: %ld ms\n", duration.count());
    
    // Calculate GFLOPS
    double gflops = (2.0 * N * N * N) / (duration.count() / 1000.0) / 1e9;
    printf("Performance: %.3f GFLOPS\n", gflops);
    
    // Print results for external validation
    if (printResults) {
        print_results(C, "MatrixC");
    }
    
    // Validation
    if (validate) {
        printf("Validating result...\n");
        bool valid = validateResult(A, B, C, N);
        
        if (valid) {
            printf("Validation: PASSED\n");
            return 0;
        } else {
            printf("Validation: FAILED\n");
            return 1;
        }
    }
    
    return 0;
}

codex
The baseline is a single-process CPU triple loop. I’m structuring the replacement so MPI partitions output rows across ranks, each rank runs a tiled CUDA kernel on its assigned GPU, and OpenMP accelerates host-side initialization and validation. Only rank 0 will retain the benchmark’s user-facing output, while elapsed time will be the slowest-rank time so the reported aggregate GFLOPS is meaningful.

+void initMatrixRows(double* matrix, const size_t n, const size_t firstRow,
+                    const size_t rows) {
+#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)
+    for (long long localRow = 0; localRow < static_cast<long long>(rows);
+         ++localRow) {
+        const size_t globalRow = firstRow + static_cast<size_t>(localRow);
+        for (size_t column = 0; column < n; ++column) {
+            matrix[static_cast<size_t>(localRow) * n + column] =
+                getPseudoRndValue(n, globalRow, column);

     }

-void matrixMultiply(const std::vector<double>& A, const std::vector<double>& B, 
-                    std::vector<double>& C, const size_t N) {
-    for (size_t i = 0; i < N; ++i) {
-        for (size_t j = 0; j < N; ++j) {
-            double sum = 0.0;
-            for (size_t k = 0; k < N; ++k) {
-                sum += A[i * N + k] * B[k * N + j];
+void initMatrix(double* matrix, const size_t n) {
+    initMatrixRows(matrix, n, 0, n);
+}
+
+// A 32x32 output tile is computed by 256 threads. Each thread accumulates four
+// output rows, increasing arithmetic intensity while retaining coalesced loads.
+__global__ void matrixMultiplyKernel(const double* __restrict__ a,
+                                     const double* __restrict__ b,
+                                     double* __restrict__ c, const int n,
+                                     const int localRows) {
+    __shared__ double aTile[kTile][kTile + 1];
+    __shared__ double bTile[kTile][kTile + 1];
+
+    const int lane = threadIdx.x;
+    const int threadRow = threadIdx.y;
+    const int column = blockIdx.x * kTile + lane;
+    const int rowBase = blockIdx.y * kTile + threadRow;
+    double sums[kTile / kThreadRows] = {0.0, 0.0, 0.0, 0.0};
+
+    for (int tileBase = 0; tileBase < n; tileBase += kTile) {
+#pragma unroll
+        for (int item = 0; item < kTile / kThreadRows; ++item) {
+            const int row = rowBase + item * kThreadRows;
+            const int tileRow = threadRow + item * kThreadRows;
+            aTile[tileRow][lane] =
+                (row < localRows && tileBase + lane < n)
+                    ? a[static_cast<size_t>(row) * n + tileBase + lane]
+                    : 0.0;
+            bTile[tileRow][lane] =
+                (tileBase + tileRow < n && column < n)
+                    ? b[static_cast<size_t>(tileBase + tileRow) * n + column]
+                    : 0.0;
+        }
+        __syncthreads();
+
+#pragma unroll
+        for (int k = 0; k < kTile; ++k) {
+            const double bValue = bTile[k][lane];
+#pragma unroll
+            for (int item = 0; item < kTile / kThreadRows; ++item) {
+                sums[item] = fma(aTile[threadRow + item * kThreadRows][k],
+                                 bValue, sums[item]);
             }
-            C[i * N + j] = sum;
         }
+        __syncthreads();
+    }
+
+    if (column < n) {
+#pragma unroll
+        for (int item = 0; item < kTile / kThreadRows; ++item) {
+            const int row = rowBase + item * kThreadRows;
+            if (row < localRows) {
+                c[static_cast<size_t>(row) * n + column] = sums[item];
+            }
+        }
     }
 }

+void abortWithMessage(const int rank, const char* message) {
+    if (rank == 0) {

+    }

+void checkCuda(const cudaError_t status, const char* operation, const int rank) {
+    if (status != cudaSuccess) {
+        std::fprintf(stderr, "Rank %d: CUDA error during %s: %s\n", rank,
+                     operation, cudaGetErrorString(status));
+        MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, EXIT_FAILURE);

+void initMatrixRows(double* matrix, const size_t n, const size_t firstRow,
+                    const size_t rows) {
+#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)
+    for (long long localRow = 0; localRow < static_cast<long long>(rows);
+         ++localRow) {
+        const size_t globalRow = firstRow + static_cast<size_t>(localRow);
+        for (size_t column = 0; column < n; ++column) {
+            matrix[static_cast<size_t>(localRow) * n + column] =
+                getPseudoRndValue(n, globalRow, column);
         }
     }

-void matrixMultiply(const std::vector<double>& A, const std::vector<double>& B, 
-                    std::vector<double>& C, const size_t N) {
-    for (size_t i = 0; i < N; ++i) {
-        for (size_t j = 0; j < N; ++j) {
-            double sum = 0.0;
-            for (size_t k = 0; k < N; ++k) {
-                sum += A[i * N + k] * B[k * N + j];
+void initMatrix(double* matrix, const size_t n) {
+    initMatrixRows(matrix, n, 0, n);
+}
+
+// A 32x32 output tile is computed by 256 threads. Each thread accumulates four
+// output rows, increasing arithmetic intensity while retaining coalesced loads.
+__global__ void matrixMultiplyKernel(const double* __restrict__ a,
+                                     const double* __restrict__ b,
+                                     double* __restrict__ c, const int n,
+                                     const int localRows) {
+    __shared__ double aTile[kTile][kTile + 1];
+    __shared__ double bTile[kTile][kTile + 1];
+
+    const int lane = threadIdx.x;
+    const int threadRow = threadIdx.y;
+    const int column = blockIdx.x * kTile + lane;
+    const int rowBase = blockIdx.y * kTile + threadRow;
+    double sums[kTile / kThreadRows] = {0.0, 0.0, 0.0, 0.0};
+
+    for (int tileBase = 0; tileBase < n; tileBase += kTile) {
+#pragma unroll
+        for (int item = 0; item < kTile / kThreadRows; ++item) {
+            const int row = rowBase + item * kThreadRows;
+            const int tileRow = threadRow + item * kThreadRows;
+            aTile[tileRow][lane] =
+                (row < localRows && tileBase + lane < n)
+                    ? a[static_cast<size_t>(row) * n + tileBase + lane]
+                    : 0.0;
+            bTile[tileRow][lane] =
+                (tileBase + tileRow < n && column < n)
+                    ? b[static_cast<size_t>(tileBase + tileRow) * n + column]
+                    : 0.0;
+        }
+        __syncthreads();
+
+#pragma unroll
+        for (int k = 0; k < kTile; ++k) {
+            const double bValue = bTile[k][lane];
+#pragma unroll
+            for (int item = 0; item < kTile / kThreadRows; ++item) {
+                sums[item] = fma(aTile[threadRow + item * kThreadRows][k],
+                                 bValue, sums[item]);
             }
-            C[i * N + j] = sum;
         }
+        __syncthreads();
+    }
+
+    if (column < n) {
+#pragma unroll
+        for (int item = 0; item < kTile / kThreadRows; ++item) {
+            const int row = rowBase + item * kThreadRows;
+            if (row < localRows) {
+                c[static_cast<size_t>(row) * n + column] = sums[item];
+            }
+        }
     }
 }

+                    MPI_COMM_WORLD);
     }
-    
-    // Validation
-    if (validate) {

-        bool valid = validateResult(A, B, C, N);
-        
-        if (valid) {
-            printf("Validation: PASSED\n");
-            return 0;
-        } else {
-            printf("Validation: FAILED\n");
-            return 1;
+
+    checkCuda(cudaFree(deviceC), "C release", rank);
+    checkCuda(cudaFree(deviceA), "A release", rank);
+    checkCuda(cudaFree(deviceB), "B release", rank);
+    MPI_Comm_free(&localCommunicator);
+
+    int returnCode = EXIT_SUCCESS;
+    if (rank == 0) {
+        const long long milliseconds =
+            static_cast<long long>(elapsedSeconds * 1000.0);
+        std::printf("Computation time: %lld ms\n", milliseconds);
+        const double operations = 2.0 * static_cast<double>(n) *
+                                  static_cast<double>(n) *
+                                  static_cast<double>(n);
+        const double gflops = elapsedSeconds > 0.0
+                                  ? operations / elapsedSeconds / 1.0e9
+                                  : std::numeric_limits<double>::infinity();
+        std::printf("Performance: %.3f GFLOPS\n", gflops);
+
+        if (printResults) {
+            print_results(fullC, "MatrixC");
         }
+        if (validate) {
+            std::printf("Validating result...\n");
+            if (validateResult(fullC, n)) {
+                std::printf("Validation: PASSED\n");
+            } else {
+                std::printf("Validation: FAILED\n");
+                returnCode = EXIT_FAILURE;
+            }
+        }
     }
-    
-    return 0;
+
+    MPI_Bcast(&returnCode, 1, MPI_INT, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+    MPI_Finalize();
+    return returnCode;
 }

+void initMatrixRows(double* matrix, const size_t n, const size_t firstRow,
+                    const size_t rows) {
+#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)
+    for (long long localRow = 0; localRow < static_cast<long long>(rows);
+         ++localRow) {
+        const size_t globalRow = firstRow + static_cast<size_t>(localRow);
+        for (size_t column = 0; column < n; ++column) {
+            matrix[static_cast<size_t>(localRow) * n + column] =
+                getPseudoRndValue(n, globalRow, column);

-void matrixMultiply(const std::vector<double>& A, const std::vector<double>& B, 
-                    std::vector<double>& C, const size_t N) {
-    for (size_t i = 0; i < N; ++i) {
-        for (size_t j = 0; j < N; ++j) {
-            double sum = 0.0;
-            for (size_t k = 0; k < N; ++k) {
-                sum += A[i * N + k] * B[k * N + j];
+void initMatrix(double* matrix, const size_t n) {
+    initMatrixRows(matrix, n, 0, n);
+}
+
+// A 32x32 output tile is computed by 256 threads. Each thread accumulates four
+// output rows, increasing arithmetic intensity while retaining coalesced loads.
+__global__ void matrixMultiplyKernel(const double* __restrict__ a,
+                                     const double* __restrict__ b,
+                                     double* __restrict__ c, const int n,
+                                     const int localRows) {
+    __shared__ double aTile[kTile][kTile + 1];
+    __shared__ double bTile[kTile][kTile + 1];
+
+    const int lane = threadIdx.x;
+    const int threadRow = threadIdx.y;
+    const int column = blockIdx.x * kTile + lane;
+    const int rowBase = blockIdx.y * kTile + threadRow;
+    double sums[kTile / kThreadRows] = {0.0, 0.0, 0.0, 0.0};
+
+    for (int tileBase = 0; tileBase < n; tileBase += kTile) {
+#pragma unroll
+        for (int item = 0; item < kTile / kThreadRows; ++item) {
+            const int row = rowBase + item * kThreadRows;
+            const int tileRow = threadRow + item * kThreadRows;
+            aTile[tileRow][lane] =
+                (row < localRows && tileBase + lane < n)
+                    ? a[static_cast<size_t>(row) * n + tileBase + lane]
+                    : 0.0;
+            bTile[tileRow][lane] =
+                (tileBase + tileRow < n && column < n)
+                    ? b[static_cast<size_t>(tileBase + tileRow) * n + column]
+                    : 0.0;
+        }
+        __syncthreads();
+
+#pragma unroll
+        for (int k = 0; k < kTile; ++k) {
+            const double bValue = bTile[k][lane];
+#pragma unroll
+            for (int item = 0; item < kTile / kThreadRows; ++item) {
+                sums[item] = fma(aTile[threadRow + item * kThreadRows][k],
+                                 bValue, sums[item]);
             }
-            C[i * N + j] = sum;
         }
+        __syncthreads();
+    }
+
+    if (column < n) {
+#pragma unroll
+        for (int item = 0; item < kTile / kThreadRows; ++item) {
+            const int row = rowBase + item * kThreadRows;
+            if (row < localRows) {
+                c[static_cast<size_t>(row) * n + column] = sums[item];
+            }
+        }
     }
 }

+void initMatrixRows(double* matrix, const size_t n, const size_t firstRow,
+                    const size_t rows) {
+#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)
+    for (long long localRow = 0; localRow < static_cast<long long>(rows);
+         ++localRow) {
+        const size_t globalRow = firstRow + static_cast<size_t>(localRow);
+        for (size_t column = 0; column < n; ++column) {

-void matrixMultiply(const std::vector<double>& A, const std::vector<double>& B,

+void initMatrix(double* matrix, const size_t n) {
+    initMatrixRows(matrix, n, 0, n);
+}
+
+// A 32x32 output tile is computed by 256 threads. Each thread accumulates four
+// output rows, increasing arithmetic intensity while retaining coalesced loads.
+__global__ void matrixMultiplyKernel(const double* __restrict__ a,
+                                     const double* __restrict__ b,
+                                     double* __restrict__ c, const int n,
+                                     const int localRows) {
+    __shared__ double aTile[kTile][kTile + 1];
+    __shared__ double bTile[kTile][kTile + 1];
+
+    const int lane = threadIdx.x;
+    const int threadRow = threadIdx.y;
+    const int column = blockIdx.x * kTile + lane;
+    const int rowBase = blockIdx.y * kTile + threadRow;
+    double sums[kTile / kThreadRows] = {0.0, 0.0, 0.0, 0.0};
+
+    for (int tileBase = 0; tileBase < n; tileBase += kTile) {
+#pragma unroll
+        for (int item = 0; item < kTile / kThreadRows; ++item) {
+            const int row = rowBase + item * kThreadRows;
+            const int tileRow = threadRow + item * kThreadRows;
+            aTile[tileRow][lane] =
+                (row < localRows && tileBase + lane < n)
+                    ? a[static_cast<size_t>(row) * n + tileBase + lane]
+                    : 0.0;
+            bTile[tileRow][lane] =
+                (tileBase + tileRow < n && column < n)
+                    ? b[static_cast<size_t>(tileBase + tileRow) * n + column]
+                    : 0.0;
+        }
+        __syncthreads();
+
+#pragma unroll
+        for (int k = 0; k < kTile; ++k) {
+            const double bValue = bTile[k][lane];
+#pragma unroll
+            for (int item = 0; item < kTile / kThreadRows; ++item) {
+                sums[item] = fma(aTile[threadRow + item * kThreadRows][k],
+                                 bValue, sums[item]);
             }
-            C[i * N + j] = sum;
         }
+        __syncthreads();
     }
+
+    if (column < n) {
+#pragma unroll
+        for (int item = 0; item < kTile / kThreadRows; ++item) {
+            const int row = rowBase + item * kThreadRows;
+            if (row < localRows) {
+                c[static_cast<size_t>(row) * n + column] = sums[item];
+            }
+        }
+    }
 }

+            for (int process = 0; process < rankCount; ++process) {
+                const size_t processRows =
+                    baseRows + (static_cast<size_t>(process) < extraRows);
+                const size_t processStart = static_cast<size_t>(process) * baseRows +
+                    std::min(static_cast<size_t>(process), extraRows);
+                if (processRows * n > static_cast<size_t>(INT_MAX) ||
+                    processStart * n > static_cast<size_t>(INT_MAX)) {
+                    abortWithMessage(rank,
+                        "Result is too large for this MPI implementation's Gatherv");
+                }
+                receiveCounts[process] = static_cast<int>(processRows * n);
+                displacements[process] = static_cast<int>(processStart * n);
+            }
+        }
+        if (localElements > static_cast<size_t>(INT_MAX)) {
+            abortWithMessage(rank,
+                "Local result is too large for this MPI implementation's Gatherv");
+        }
+        MPI_Gatherv(localC.data(), static_cast<int>(localElements), MPI_DOUBLE,
+                    rank == 0 ? fullC.data() : nullptr,
+                    rank == 0 ? receiveCounts.data() : nullptr,
+                    rank == 0 ? displacements.data() : nullptr, MPI_DOUBLE, 0,
+                    MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+    }
+
+    checkCuda(cudaFree(deviceC), "C release", rank);
+    checkCuda(cudaFree(deviceA), "A release", rank);
+    checkCuda(cudaFree(deviceB), "B release", rank);
+    MPI_Comm_free(&localCommunicator);
+
+    int returnCode = EXIT_SUCCESS;
+    if (rank == 0) {
+        const long long milliseconds =
+            static_cast<long long>(elapsedSeconds * 1000.0);
+        std::printf("Computation time: %lld ms\n", milliseconds);
+        const double operations = 2.0 * static_cast<double>(n) *
+                                  static_cast<double>(n) *
+                                  static_cast<double>(n);
+        const double gflops = elapsedSeconds > 0.0
+                                  ? operations / elapsedSeconds / 1.0e9
+                                  : std::numeric_limits<double>::infinity();
+        std::printf("Performance: %.3f GFLOPS\n", gflops);
+
+        if (printResults) {
+            print_results(fullC, "MatrixC");
         }
+        if (validate) {
+            std::printf("Validating result...\n");
+            if (validateResult(fullC, n)) {
+                std::printf("Validation: PASSED\n");
+            } else {
+                std::printf("Validation: FAILED\n");
+                returnCode = EXIT_FAILURE;
+            }
+        }
     }
-    
-    return 0;
+
+    MPI_Bcast(&returnCode, 1, MPI_INT, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+    MPI_Finalize();
+    return returnCode;
 }

+void initMatrixRows(double* matrix, const size_t n, const size_t firstRow,
+                    const size_t rows) {
+#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)
+    for (long long localRow = 0; localRow < static_cast<long long>(rows);
+         ++localRow) {
+        const size_t globalRow = firstRow + static_cast<size_t>(localRow);
+        for (size_t column = 0; column < n; ++column) {
+            matrix[static_cast<size_t>(localRow) * n + column] =
+                getPseudoRndValue(n, globalRow, column);

-void matrixMultiply(const std::vector<double>& A, const std::vector<double>& B, 
-                    std::vector<double>& C, const size_t N) {
-    for (size_t i = 0; i < N; ++i) {
-        for (size_t j = 0; j < N; ++j) {
-            double sum = 0.0;
-            for (size_t k = 0; k < N; ++k) {
-                sum += A[i * N + k] * B[k * N + j];
+void initMatrix(double* matrix, const size_t n) {
+    initMatrixRows(matrix, n, 0, n);
+}
+
+// A 32x32 output tile is computed by 256 threads. Each thread accumulates four
+// output rows, increasing arithmetic intensity while retaining coalesced loads.
+__global__ void matrixMultiplyKernel(const double* __restrict__ a,
+                                     const double* __restrict__ b,
+                                     double* __restrict__ c, const int n,
+                                     const int localRows) {
+    __shared__ double aTile[kTile][kTile + 1];
+    __shared__ double bTile[kTile][kTile + 1];
+
+    const int lane = threadIdx.x;
+    const int threadRow = threadIdx.y;
+    const int column = blockIdx.x * kTile + lane;
+    const int rowBase = blockIdx.y * kTile + threadRow;
+    double sums[kTile / kThreadRows] = {0.0, 0.0, 0.0, 0.0};
+
+    for (int tileBase = 0; tileBase < n; tileBase += kTile) {
+#pragma unroll
+        for (int item = 0; item < kTile / kThreadRows; ++item) {
+            const int row = rowBase + item * kThreadRows;
+            const int tileRow = threadRow + item * kThreadRows;
+            aTile[tileRow][lane] =
+                (row < localRows && tileBase + lane < n)
+                    ? a[static_cast<size_t>(row) * n + tileBase + lane]
+                    : 0.0;
+            bTile[tileRow][lane] =
+                (tileBase + tileRow < n && column < n)
+                    ? b[static_cast<size_t>(tileBase + tileRow) * n + column]
+                    : 0.0;
+        }
+        __syncthreads();
+
+#pragma unroll
+        for (int k = 0; k < kTile; ++k) {
+            const double bValue = bTile[k][lane];
+#pragma unroll
+            for (int item = 0; item < kTile / kThreadRows; ++item) {
+                sums[item] = fma(aTile[threadRow + item * kThreadRows][k],
+                                 bValue, sums[item]);
             }
-            C[i * N + j] = sum;
         }
+        __syncthreads();
     }
+
+    if (column < n) {
+#pragma unroll
+        for (int item = 0; item < kTile / kThreadRows; ++item) {
+            const int row = rowBase + item * kThreadRows;
+            if (row < localRows) {
+                c[static_cast<size_t>(row) * n + column] = sums[item];
+            }
+        }
+    }
 }

+void checkCuda(const cudaError_t status, const char* operation, const int rank) {
+    if (status != cudaSuccess) {
+        std::fprintf(stderr, "Rank %d: CUDA error during %s: %s\n", rank,

+        MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, EXIT_FAILURE);

+void initMatrixRows(double* matrix, const size_t n, const size_t firstRow,
+                    const size_t rows) {
+#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)
+    for (long long localRow = 0; localRow < static_cast<long long>(rows);
+         ++localRow) {
+        const size_t globalRow = firstRow + static_cast<size_t>(localRow);
+        for (size_t column = 0; column < n; ++column) {
+            matrix[static_cast<size_t>(localRow) * n + column] =
+                getPseudoRndValue(n, globalRow, column);
         }
     }
 }
 
-void matrixMultiply(const std::vector<double>& A, const std::vector<double>& B, 
-                    std::vector<double>& C, const size_t N) {
-    for (size_t i = 0; i < N; ++i) {
-        for (size_t j = 0; j < N; ++j) {

-            for (size_t k = 0; k < N; ++k) {
-                sum += A[i * N + k] * B[k * N + j];
+void initMatrix(double* matrix, const size_t n) {
+    initMatrixRows(matrix, n, 0, n);
+}
+
+// A 32x32 output tile is computed by 256 threads. Each thread accumulates four
+// output rows, increasing arithmetic intensity while retaining coalesced loads.
+__global__ void matrixMultiplyKernel(const double* __restrict__ a,
+                                     const double* __restrict__ b,
+                                     double* __restrict__ c, const int n,
+                                     const int localRows) {
+    __shared__ double aTile[kTile][kTile + 1];
+    __shared__ double bTile[kTile][kTile + 1];
+
+    const int lane = threadIdx.x;
+    const int threadRow = threadIdx.y;
+    const int column = blockIdx.x * kTile + lane;
+    const int rowBase = blockIdx.y * kTile + threadRow;
+    double sums[kTile / kThreadRows] = {0.0, 0.0, 0.0, 0.0};
+
+    for (int tileBase = 0; tileBase < n; tileBase += kTile) {
+#pragma unroll
+        for (int item = 0; item < kTile / kThreadRows; ++item) {
+            const int row = rowBase + item * kThreadRows;
+            const int tileRow = threadRow + item * kThreadRows;
+            aTile[tileRow][lane] =
+                (row < localRows && tileBase + lane < n)
+                    ? a[static_cast<size_t>(row) * n + tileBase + lane]
+                    : 0.0;
+            bTile[tileRow][lane] =
+                (tileBase + tileRow < n && column < n)
+                    ? b[static_cast<size_t>(tileBase + tileRow) * n + column]
+                    : 0.0;
+        }
+        __syncthreads();
+
+#pragma unroll
+        for (int k = 0; k < kTile; ++k) {
+            const double bValue = bTile[k][lane];
+#pragma unroll
+            for (int item = 0; item < kTile / kThreadRows; ++item) {
+                sums[item] = fma(aTile[threadRow + item * kThreadRows][k],
+                                 bValue, sums[item]);
             }
-            C[i * N + j] = sum;
         }
+        __syncthreads();
     }
+
+    if (column < n) {
+#pragma unroll
+        for (int item = 0; item < kTile / kThreadRows; ++item) {
+            const int row = rowBase + item * kThreadRows;
+            if (row < localRows) {
+                c[static_cast<size_t>(row) * n + column] = sums[item];
+            }
+        }
+    }
 }

+void initMatrixRows(double* matrix, const size_t n, const size_t firstRow,
+                    const size_t rows) {
+#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)
+    for (long long localRow = 0; localRow < static_cast<long long>(rows);
+         ++localRow) {
+        const size_t globalRow = firstRow + static_cast<size_t>(localRow);
+        for (size_t column = 0; column < n; ++column) {

-void matrixMultiply(const std::vector<double>& A, const std::vector<double>& B,

+void initMatrix(double* matrix, const size_t n) {
+    initMatrixRows(matrix, n, 0, n);
+}
+
+// A 32x32 output tile is computed by 256 threads. Each thread accumulates four
+// output rows, increasing arithmetic intensity while retaining coalesced loads.
+__global__ void matrixMultiplyKernel(const double* __restrict__ a,
+                                     const double* __restrict__ b,
+                                     double* __restrict__ c, const int n,
+                                     const int localRows) {
+    __shared__ double aTile[kTile][kTile + 1];
+    __shared__ double bTile[kTile][kTile + 1];
+
+    const int lane = threadIdx.x;
+    const int threadRow = threadIdx.y;
+    const int column = blockIdx.x * kTile + lane;
+    const int rowBase = blockIdx.y * kTile + threadRow;
+    double sums[kTile / kThreadRows] = {0.0, 0.0, 0.0, 0.0};
+
+    for (int tileBase = 0; tileBase < n; tileBase += kTile) {
+#pragma unroll
+        for (int item = 0; item < kTile / kThreadRows; ++item) {
+            const int row = rowBase + item * kThreadRows;
+            const int tileRow = threadRow + item * kThreadRows;
+            aTile[tileRow][lane] =
+                (row < localRows && tileBase + lane < n)
+                    ? a[static_cast<size_t>(row) * n + tileBase + lane]
+                    : 0.0;
+            bTile[tileRow][lane] =
+                (tileBase + tileRow < n && column < n)
+                    ? b[static_cast<size_t>(tileBase + tileRow) * n + column]
+                    : 0.0;
+        }
+        __syncthreads();
+
+#pragma unroll
+        for (int k = 0; k < kTile; ++k) {
+            const double bValue = bTile[k][lane];
+#pragma unroll
+            for (int item = 0; item < kTile / kThreadRows; ++item) {
+                sums[item] = fma(aTile[threadRow + item * kThreadRows][k],
+                                 bValue, sums[item]);
             }
-            C[i * N + j] = sum;
         }
+        __syncthreads();
     }
+
+    if (column < n) {
+#pragma unroll
+        for (int item = 0; item < kTile / kThreadRows; ++item) {
+            const int row = rowBase + item * kThreadRows;
+            if (row < localRows) {
+                c[static_cast<size_t>(row) * n + column] = sums[item];
+            }
+        }
+    }
 }

Configuration confirms the environment provides all three required toolchains (OpenMPI, OpenMP, and CUDA 12). The implementation now builds them as required components, so it cannot silently fall back to a serial or CPU-only path. I’m compiling next and will address any CUDA/MPI integration issues before running correctness checks.

+        std::fprintf(stderr, "%s\n", message);
+    }
+    MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, EXIT_FAILURE);
+}
+
+void checkCuda(const cudaError_t status, const char* operation, const int rank) {
+    if (status != cudaSuccess) {
+        std::fprintf(stderr, "Rank %d: CUDA error during %s: %s\n", rank,
+                     operation, cudaGetErrorString(status));
+        MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, EXIT_FAILURE);
+    }
+}
+
+void initMatrixRows(double* matrix, const size_t n, const size_t firstRow,
+                    const size_t rows) {
+#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)
+    for (long long localRow = 0; localRow < static_cast<long long>(rows);
+         ++localRow) {
+        const size_t globalRow = firstRow + static_cast<size_t>(localRow);
+        for (size_t column = 0; column < n; ++column) {
+            matrix[static_cast<size_t>(localRow) * n + column] =
+                getPseudoRndValue(n, globalRow, column);
         }
     }
 }
 
-void matrixMultiply(const std::vector<double>& A, const std::vector<double>& B, 
-                    std::vector<double>& C, const size_t N) {
-    for (size_t i = 0; i < N; ++i) {
-        for (size_t j = 0; j < N; ++j) {

-            for (size_t k = 0; k < N; ++k) {
-                sum += A[i * N + k] * B[k * N + j];
+void initMatrix(double* matrix, const size_t n) {
+    initMatrixRows(matrix, n, 0, n);
+}
+
+// A 32x32 output tile is computed by 256 threads. Each thread accumulates four
+// output rows, increasing arithmetic intensity while retaining coalesced loads.
+__global__ void matrixMultiplyKernel(const double* __restrict__ a,
+                                     const double* __restrict__ b,
+                                     double* __restrict__ c, const int n,
+                                     const int localRows) {
+    __shared__ double aTile[kTile][kTile + 1];
+    __shared__ double bTile[kTile][kTile + 1];
+
+    const int lane = threadIdx.x;
+    const int threadRow = threadIdx.y;
+    const int column = blockIdx.x * kTile + lane;
+    const int rowBase = blockIdx.y * kTile + threadRow;
+    double sums[kTile / kThreadRows] = {0.0, 0.0, 0.0, 0.0};
+
+    for (int tileBase = 0; tileBase < n; tileBase += kTile) {
+#pragma unroll
+        for (int item = 0; item < kTile / kThreadRows; ++item) {
+            const int row = rowBase + item * kThreadRows;
+            const int tileRow = threadRow + item * kThreadRows;
+            aTile[tileRow][lane] =
+                (row < localRows && tileBase + lane < n)
+                    ? a[static_cast<size_t>(row) * n + tileBase + lane]
+                    : 0.0;
+            bTile[tileRow][lane] =
+                (tileBase + tileRow < n && column < n)
+                    ? b[static_cast<size_t>(tileBase + tileRow) * n + column]
+                    : 0.0;
+        }
+        __syncthreads();
+
+#pragma unroll
+        for (int k = 0; k < kTile; ++k) {
+            const double bValue = bTile[k][lane];
+#pragma unroll
+            for (int item = 0; item < kTile / kThreadRows; ++item) {
+                sums[item] = fma(aTile[threadRow + item * kThreadRows][k],
+                                 bValue, sums[item]);
             }
-            C[i * N + j] = sum;
         }
+        __syncthreads();
     }
+
+    if (column < n) {
+#pragma unroll
+        for (int item = 0; item < kTile / kThreadRows; ++item) {
+            const int row = rowBase + item * kThreadRows;
+            if (row < localRows) {
+                c[static_cast<size_t>(row) * n + column] = sums[item];
+            }
+        }
+    }
 }

+
+// A 32x32 output tile is computed by 256 threads. Each thread accumulates four
+// output rows, increasing arithmetic intensity while retaining coalesced loads.
+__global__ void matrixMultiplyKernel(const double* __restrict__ a,
+                                     const double* __restrict__ b,
+                                     double* __restrict__ c, const int n,
+                                     const int localRows) {
+    __shared__ double aTile[kTile][kTile + 1];
+    __shared__ double bTile[kTile][kTile + 1];
+
+    const int lane = threadIdx.x;
+    const int threadRow = threadIdx.y;
+    const int column = blockIdx.x * kTile + lane;
+    const int rowBase = blockIdx.y * kTile + threadRow;
+    double sums[kTile / kThreadRows] = {0.0, 0.0, 0.0, 0.0};
+
+    for (int tileBase = 0; tileBase < n; tileBase += kTile) {
+#pragma unroll
+        for (int item = 0; item < kTile / kThreadRows; ++item) {
+            const int row = rowBase + item * kThreadRows;
+            const int tileRow = threadRow + item * kThreadRows;
+            aTile[tileRow][lane] =
+                (row < localRows && tileBase + lane < n)
+                    ? a[static_cast<size_t>(row) * n + tileBase + lane]
+                    : 0.0;
+            bTile[tileRow][lane] =
+                (tileBase + tileRow < n && column < n)
+                    ? b[static_cast<size_t>(tileBase + tileRow) * n + column]
+                    : 0.0;
+        }
+        __syncthreads();
+
+#pragma unroll
+        for (int k = 0; k < kTile; ++k) {
+            const double bValue = bTile[k][lane];
+#pragma unroll
+            for (int item = 0; item < kTile / kThreadRows; ++item) {
+                sums[item] = fma(aTile[threadRow + item * kThreadRows][k],
+                                 bValue, sums[item]);
             }
-            C[i * N + j] = sum;
         }
+        __syncthreads();
     }
+
+    if (column < n) {
+#pragma unroll
+        for (int item = 0; item < kTile / kThreadRows; ++item) {
+            const int row = rowBase + item * kThreadRows;
+            if (row < localRows) {
+                c[static_cast<size_t>(row) * n + column] = sums[item];
+            }
+        }
+    }

+void abortWithMessage(const int rank, const char* message) {
+    if (rank == 0) {
+        std::fprintf(stderr, "%s\n", message);
+    }
+    MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, EXIT_FAILURE);

+void checkCuda(const cudaError_t status, const char* operation, const int rank) {
+    if (status != cudaSuccess) {
+        std::fprintf(stderr, "Rank %d: CUDA error during %s: %s\n", rank,
+                     operation, cudaGetErrorString(status));
+        MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, EXIT_FAILURE);

+void initMatrixRows(double* matrix, const size_t n, const size_t firstRow,
+                    const size_t rows) {
+#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)
+    for (long long localRow = 0; localRow < static_cast<long long>(rows);
+         ++localRow) {
+        const size_t globalRow = firstRow + static_cast<size_t>(localRow);
+        for (size_t column = 0; column < n; ++column) {
+            matrix[static_cast<size_t>(localRow) * n + column] =
+                getPseudoRndValue(n, globalRow, column);
         }
     }

-void matrixMultiply(const std::vector<double>& A, const std::vector<double>& B, 
-                    std::vector<double>& C, const size_t N) {
-    for (size_t i = 0; i < N; ++i) {
-        for (size_t j = 0; j < N; ++j) {
-            double sum = 0.0;
-            for (size_t k = 0; k < N; ++k) {
-                sum += A[i * N + k] * B[k * N + j];
+void initMatrix(double* matrix, const size_t n) {
+    initMatrixRows(matrix, n, 0, n);
+}
+
+// A 32x32 output tile is computed by 256 threads. Each thread accumulates four
+// output rows, increasing arithmetic intensity while retaining coalesced loads.
+__global__ void matrixMultiplyKernel(const double* __restrict__ a,
+                                     const double* __restrict__ b,
+                                     double* __restrict__ c, const int n,
+                                     const int localRows) {
+    __shared__ double aTile[kTile][kTile + 1];
+    __shared__ double bTile[kTile][kTile + 1];
+
+    const int lane = threadIdx.x;
+    const int threadRow = threadIdx.y;
+    const int column = blockIdx.x * kTile + lane;
+    const int rowBase = blockIdx.y * kTile + threadRow;
+    double sums[kTile / kThreadRows] = {0.0, 0.0, 0.0, 0.0};
+
+    for (int tileBase = 0; tileBase < n; tileBase += kTile) {
+#pragma unroll
+        for (int item = 0; item < kTile / kThreadRows; ++item) {
+            const int row = rowBase + item * kThreadRows;
+            const int tileRow = threadRow + item * kThreadRows;
+            aTile[tileRow][lane] =
+                (row < localRows && tileBase + lane < n)
+                    ? a[static_cast<size_t>(row) * n + tileBase + lane]
+                    : 0.0;
+            bTile[tileRow][lane] =
+                (tileBase + tileRow < n && column < n)
+                    ? b[static_cast<size_t>(tileBase + tileRow) * n + column]
+                    : 0.0;
+        }
+        __syncthreads();
+
+#pragma unroll
+        for (int k = 0; k < kTile; ++k) {
+            const double bValue = bTile[k][lane];
+#pragma unroll
+            for (int item = 0; item < kTile / kThreadRows; ++item) {
+                sums[item] = fma(aTile[threadRow + item * kThreadRows][k],
+                                 bValue, sums[item]);
             }
-            C[i * N + j] = sum;
         }
+        __syncthreads();
     }
+
+    if (column < n) {
+#pragma unroll
+        for (int item = 0; item < kTile / kThreadRows; ++item) {
+            const int row = rowBase + item * kThreadRows;
+            if (row < localRows) {
+                c[static_cast<size_t>(row) * n + column] = sums[item];
+            }
+        }
+    }
 }

-            
+    long long failedCheck = -1;

+void checkCuda(const cudaError_t status, const char* operation, const int rank) {
+    if (status != cudaSuccess) {
+        std::fprintf(stderr, "Rank %d: CUDA error during %s: %s\n", rank,

+void initMatrixRows(double* matrix, const size_t n, const size_t firstRow,
+                    const size_t rows) {
+#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)
+    for (long long localRow = 0; localRow < static_cast<long long>(rows);
+         ++localRow) {
+        const size_t globalRow = firstRow + static_cast<size_t>(localRow);
+        for (size_t column = 0; column < n; ++column) {

-void matrixMultiply(const std::vector<double>& A, const std::vector<double>& B, 
-                    std::vector<double>& C, const size_t N) {
-    for (size_t i = 0; i < N; ++i) {
-        for (size_t j = 0; j < N; ++j) {
-            double sum = 0.0;
-            for (size_t k = 0; k < N; ++k) {
-                sum += A[i * N + k] * B[k * N + j];
+void initMatrix(double* matrix, const size_t n) {
+    initMatrixRows(matrix, n, 0, n);
+}
+
+// A 32x32 output tile is computed by 256 threads. Each thread accumulates four
+// output rows, increasing arithmetic intensity while retaining coalesced loads.
+__global__ void matrixMultiplyKernel(const double* __restrict__ a,
+                                     const double* __restrict__ b,
+                                     double* __restrict__ c, const int n,
+                                     const int localRows) {
+    __shared__ double aTile[kTile][kTile + 1];
+    __shared__ double bTile[kTile][kTile + 1];
+
+    const int lane = threadIdx.x;
+    const int threadRow = threadIdx.y;
+    const int column = blockIdx.x * kTile + lane;
+    const int rowBase = blockIdx.y * kTile + threadRow;
+    double sums[kTile / kThreadRows] = {0.0, 0.0, 0.0, 0.0};
+
+    for (int tileBase = 0; tileBase < n; tileBase += kTile) {
+#pragma unroll
+        for (int item = 0; item < kTile / kThreadRows; ++item) {
+            const int row = rowBase + item * kThreadRows;
+            const int tileRow = threadRow + item * kThreadRows;
+            aTile[tileRow][lane] =
+                (row < localRows && tileBase + lane < n)
+                    ? a[static_cast<size_t>(row) * n + tileBase + lane]
+                    : 0.0;
+            bTile[tileRow][lane] =
+                (tileBase + tileRow < n && column < n)
+                    ? b[static_cast<size_t>(tileBase + tileRow) * n + column]
+                    : 0.0;
+        }
+        __syncthreads();
+
+#pragma unroll
+        for (int k = 0; k < kTile; ++k) {
+            const double bValue = bTile[k][lane];
+#pragma unroll
+            for (int item = 0; item < kTile / kThreadRows; ++item) {
+                sums[item] = fma(aTile[threadRow + item * kThreadRows][k],
+                                 bValue, sums[item]);
             }
-            C[i * N + j] = sum;
         }
+        __syncthreads();
     }
+
+    if (column < n) {
+#pragma unroll
+        for (int item = 0; item < kTile / kThreadRows; ++item) {
+            const int row = rowBase + item * kThreadRows;
+            if (row < localRows) {
+                c[static_cast<size_t>(row) * n + column] = sums[item];
+            }
+        }
+    }
 }

+void checkCuda(const cudaError_t status, const char* operation, const int rank) {
+    if (status != cudaSuccess) {
+        std::fprintf(stderr, "Rank %d: CUDA error during %s: %s\n", rank,

+void initMatrixRows(double* matrix, const size_t n, const size_t firstRow,
+                    const size_t rows) {
+#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)
+    for (long long localRow = 0; localRow < static_cast<long long>(rows);
+         ++localRow) {
+        const size_t globalRow = firstRow + static_cast<size_t>(localRow);
+        for (size_t column = 0; column < n; ++column) {
+            matrix[static_cast<size_t>(localRow) * n + column] =
+                getPseudoRndValue(n, globalRow, column);

-void matrixMultiply(const std::vector<double>& A, const std::vector<double>& B, 
-                    std::vector<double>& C, const size_t N) {
-    for (size_t i = 0; i < N; ++i) {
-        for (size_t j = 0; j < N; ++j) {
-            double sum = 0.0;
-            for (size_t k = 0; k < N; ++k) {
-                sum += A[i * N + k] * B[k * N + j];
+void initMatrix(double* matrix, const size_t n) {
+    initMatrixRows(matrix, n, 0, n);
+}
+
+// A 32x32 output tile is computed by 256 threads. Each thread accumulates four
+// output rows, increasing arithmetic intensity while retaining coalesced loads.
+__global__ void matrixMultiplyKernel(const double* __restrict__ a,
+                                     const double* __restrict__ b,
+                                     double* __restrict__ c, const int n,
+                                     const int localRows) {
+    __shared__ double aTile[kTile][kTile + 1];
+    __shared__ double bTile[kTile][kTile + 1];
+
+    const int lane = threadIdx.x;
+    const int threadRow = threadIdx.y;
+    const int column = blockIdx.x * kTile + lane;
+    const int rowBase = blockIdx.y * kTile + threadRow;
+    double sums[kTile / kThreadRows] = {0.0, 0.0, 0.0, 0.0};
+
+    for (int tileBase = 0; tileBase < n; tileBase += kTile) {
+#pragma unroll
+        for (int item = 0; item < kTile / kThreadRows; ++item) {
+            const int row = rowBase + item * kThreadRows;
+            const int tileRow = threadRow + item * kThreadRows;
+            aTile[tileRow][lane] =
+                (row < localRows && tileBase + lane < n)
+                    ? a[static_cast<size_t>(row) * n + tileBase + lane]
+                    : 0.0;
+            bTile[tileRow][lane] =
+                (tileBase + tileRow < n && column < n)
+                    ? b[static_cast<size_t>(tileBase + tileRow) * n + column]
+                    : 0.0;
+        }
+        __syncthreads();
+
+#pragma unroll
+        for (int k = 0; k < kTile; ++k) {
+            const double bValue = bTile[k][lane];
+#pragma unroll
+            for (int item = 0; item < kTile / kThreadRows; ++item) {
+                sums[item] = fma(aTile[threadRow + item * kThreadRows][k],
+                                 bValue, sums[item]);
             }
-            C[i * N + j] = sum;
         }
+        __syncthreads();
     }
+
+    if (column < n) {
+#pragma unroll
+        for (int item = 0; item < kTile / kThreadRows; ++item) {
+            const int row = rowBase + item * kThreadRows;
+            if (row < localRows) {
+                c[static_cast<size_t>(row) * n + column] = sums[item];
+            }
+        }
+    }
 }

    18	namespace {
    19	
    20	constexpr int kTile = 32;
    21	constexpr int kThreadRows = 8;
    22	
    23	// Generate pseudo-random values for matrix initialization.
    24	constexpr double getPseudoRndValue(const size_t n, const size_t i,
    25	                                   const size_t j) noexcept {
    26	    return (((i + 1) * (i + j + 1) * 1299709) % (n * n)) /
    27	           static_cast<double>(n * n);
    28	}
    29	
    30	void abortWithMessage(const int rank, const char* message) {
    31	    if (rank == 0) {
    32	        std::fprintf(stderr, "%s\n", message);
    33	    }
    34	    MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, EXIT_FAILURE);
    35	}
    36	
    37	void checkCuda(const cudaError_t status, const char* operation, const int rank) {
    38	    if (status != cudaSuccess) {
    39	        std::fprintf(stderr, "Rank %d: CUDA error during %s: %s\n", rank,
    40	                     operation, cudaGetErrorString(status));
    41	        MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, EXIT_FAILURE);
    42	    }
    43	}
    44	
    45	void initMatrixRows(double* matrix, const size_t n, const size_t firstRow,
    46	                    const size_t rows) {
    47	#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)
    48	    for (long long localRow = 0; localRow < static_cast<long long>(rows);
    49	         ++localRow) {
    50	        const size_t globalRow = firstRow + static_cast<size_t>(localRow);
    51	        for (size_t column = 0; column < n; ++column) {
    52	            matrix[static_cast<size_t>(localRow) * n + column] =
    53	                getPseudoRndValue(n, globalRow, column);
    54	        }
    55	    }
    56	}
    57	
    58	void initMatrix(double* matrix, const size_t n) {
    59	    initMatrixRows(matrix, n, 0, n);
    60	}
    61	
    62	// A 32x32 output tile is computed by 256 threads. Each thread accumulates four
    63	// output rows, increasing arithmetic intensity while retaining coalesced loads.
    64	__global__ void matrixMultiplyKernel(const double* __restrict__ a,
    65	                                     const double* __restrict__ b,
    66	                                     double* __restrict__ c, const int n,
    67	                                     const int localRows) {
    68	    __shared__ double aTile[kTile][kTile + 1];
    69	    __shared__ double bTile[kTile][kTile + 1];
    70	
    71	    const int lane = threadIdx.x;
    72	    const int threadRow = threadIdx.y;
    73	    const int column = blockIdx.x * kTile + lane;
    74	    const int rowBase = blockIdx.y * kTile + threadRow;
    75	    double sums[kTile / kThreadRows] = {0.0, 0.0, 0.0, 0.0};
    76	
    77	    for (int tileBase = 0; tileBase < n; tileBase += kTile) {
    78	#pragma unroll
    79	        for (int item = 0; item < kTile / kThreadRows; ++item) {
    80	            const int row = rowBase + item * kThreadRows;
    81	            const int tileRow = threadRow + item * kThreadRows;
    82	            aTile[tileRow][lane] =
    83	                (row < localRows && tileBase + lane < n)
    84	                    ? a[static_cast<size_t>(row) * n + tileBase + lane]
    85	                    : 0.0;
    86	            bTile[tileRow][lane] =
    87	                (tileBase + tileRow < n && column < n)
    88	                    ? b[static_cast<size_t>(tileBase + tileRow) * n + column]
    89	                    : 0.0;
    90	        }
    91	        __syncthreads();
    92	
    93	#pragma unroll
    94	        for (int k = 0; k < kTile; ++k) {
    95	            const double bValue = bTile[k][lane];
    96	#pragma unroll
    97	            for (int item = 0; item < kTile / kThreadRows; ++item) {
    98	                sums[item] = fma(aTile[threadRow + item * kThreadRows][k],
    99	                                 bValue, sums[item]);
   100	            }
   101	        }
   102	        __syncthreads();
   103	    }
   104	
   105	    if (column < n) {
   106	#pragma unroll
   107	        for (int item = 0; item < kTile / kThreadRows; ++item) {
   108	            const int row = rowBase + item * kThreadRows;
   109	            if (row < localRows) {
   110	                c[static_cast<size_t>(row) * n + column] = sums[item];
   111	            }
   112	        }
   113	    }
   114	}
   115	
   116	bool validateResult(const std::vector<double>& c, const size_t n) {
   117	    constexpr size_t checkPoints[] = {0, 1, 2, 3, 4};
   118	    long long failedCheck = -1;
   119	    double failedExpected = 0.0;
   120	    double failedActual = 0.0;
   121	    double failedError = 0.0;
   122	
   123	#pragma omp parallel for collapse(2) schedule(static)
   124	    for (int pi = 0; pi < 5; ++pi) {
   125	        for (int pj = 0; pj < 5; ++pj) {
   126	            const size_t i = checkPoints[pi] % n;
   127	            const size_t j = checkPoints[pj] % n;
   128	            double expected = 0.0;
   129	            for (size_t k = 0; k < n; ++k) {
   130	                expected += getPseudoRndValue(n, i, k) *
   131	                            getPseudoRndValue(n, k, j);
   132	            }
   133	            const double actual = c[i * n + j];
   134	            const double relativeError =
   135	                std::abs((actual - expected) / (expected + 1e-10));
   136	            if (relativeError > 1e-6) {
   137	#pragma omp critical
   138	                {
   139	                    const long long check = pi * 5LL + pj;
   140	                    if (failedCheck < 0 || check < failedCheck) {
   141	                        failedCheck = check;
   142	                        failedExpected = expected;
   143	                        failedActual = actual;
   144	                        failedError = relativeError;
   145	                    }
   146	                }
   147	            }
   148	        }
   149	    }
   150	
   151	    if (failedCheck >= 0) {
   152	        const size_t i = checkPoints[failedCheck / 5] % n;
   153	        const size_t j = checkPoints[failedCheck % 5] % n;
   154	        std::printf("Validation failed at (%zu, %zu): expected %.10f, got "
   155	                    "%.10f (error: %.10e)\n",
   156	                    i, j, failedExpected, failedActual, failedError);
   157	        return false;
   158	    }
   159	    return true;
   160	}
   161	
   162	void printUsage(const char* programName) {
   163	    std::printf("Usage: %s [options]\n", programName);
   164	    std::printf("Options:\n");
   165	    std::printf("  -n <num>     Matrix size N (computes NxN * NxN) (default: 512)\n");
   166	    std::printf("  -v           Enable validation\n");
   167	    std::printf("  -r           Print results for external validation\n");
   168	    std::printf("  -h           Show this help message\n");
   169	}

   171	}  // namespace

   183	    }

   195	            if (errno != 0 || end == argv[i] || *end != '\0' || parsed == 0 ||

   205	        } else if (std::strcmp(argv[i], "-h") == 0) {

   207	        } else {

   210	            }
   211	            argumentsValid = false;
   212	        }
   213	    }

   229	    const size_t extraRows = n % static_cast<size_t>(rankCount);
   230	    const size_t localRows = baseRows + (static_cast<size_t>(rank) < extraRows);
   231	    const size_t firstRow = static_cast<size_t>(rank) * baseRows +
   232	                            std::min(static_cast<size_t>(rank), extraRows);
   233	    const size_t localElements = localRows * n;
   234	
   235	    MPI_Comm localCommunicator = MPI_COMM_NULL;
   236	    MPI_Comm_split_type(MPI_COMM_WORLD, MPI_COMM_TYPE_SHARED, rank,
   237	                        MPI_INFO_NULL, &localCommunicator);
   238	    int localRank = 0;
   239	    MPI_Comm_rank(localCommunicator, &localRank);
   240	    int deviceCount = 0;
   241	    checkCuda(cudaGetDeviceCount(&deviceCount), "device discovery", rank);
   242	    if (deviceCount == 0) {
   243	        abortWithMessage(rank, "No CUDA device is available");
   244	    }
   245	    checkCuda(cudaSetDevice(localRank % deviceCount), "device selection", rank);
   246	
   247	    if (rank == 0) {
   248	        std::printf("Matrix Multiplication Benchmark\n");
   249	        std::printf("Matrix size: %zu x %zu\n", n, n);
   250	        std::printf("MPI ranks: %d, OpenMP threads/rank: %d\n", rankCount,
   251	                    omp_get_max_threads());
   252	        std::printf("Validation: %s\n", validate ? "enabled" : "disabled");
   253	        std::printf("Initializing matrices...\n");
   254	    }
   255	
   256	    std::vector<double> localA(localElements);
   257	    std::vector<double> b(n * n);
   258	    initMatrixRows(localA.data(), n, firstRow, localRows);
   259	    initMatrix(b.data(), n);
   260	
   261	    double* deviceA = nullptr;
   262	    double* deviceB = nullptr;
   263	    double* deviceC = nullptr;
   264	    checkCuda(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&deviceB), n * n * sizeof(double)),
   265	              "B allocation", rank);
   266	    checkCuda(cudaMemcpy(deviceB, b.data(), n * n * sizeof(double),
   267	                         cudaMemcpyHostToDevice),
   268	              "B upload", rank);
   269	    if (localElements != 0) {
   270	        checkCuda(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&deviceA),
   271	                             localElements * sizeof(double)),
   272	                  "A allocation", rank);

   274	                             localElements * sizeof(double)),
   275	                  "C allocation", rank);
   276	        checkCuda(cudaMemcpy(deviceA, localA.data(), localElements * sizeof(double),
   277	                             cudaMemcpyHostToDevice),
   278	                  "A upload", rank);
   279	    }
   280	
   281	    if (rank == 0) {
   282	        std::printf("Computing matrix multiplication...\n");
   283	    }
   284	    MPI_Barrier(MPI_COMM_WORLD);
   285	    const double start = MPI_Wtime();
   286	    if (localRows != 0) {
   287	        const dim3 threads(kTile, kThreadRows);
   288	        const dim3 blocks((static_cast<unsigned int>(n) + kTile - 1) / kTile,
   289	                          (static_cast<unsigned int>(localRows) + kTile - 1) /
   290	                              kTile);
   291	        matrixMultiplyKernel<<<blocks, threads>>>(
   292	            deviceA, deviceB, deviceC, static_cast<int>(n),
   293	            static_cast<int>(localRows));
   294	        checkCuda(cudaGetLastError(), "kernel launch", rank);
   295	        checkCuda(cudaDeviceSynchronize(), "kernel execution", rank);
   296	    }
   297	    const double localSeconds = MPI_Wtime() - start;
   298	    double elapsedSeconds = 0.0;
   299	    MPI_Reduce(&localSeconds, &elapsedSeconds, 1, MPI_DOUBLE, MPI_MAX, 0,
   300	               MPI_COMM_WORLD);
   301	
   302	    std::vector<double> fullC;
   303	    if (validate || printResults) {
   304	        std::vector<double> localC(localElements);
   305	        if (localElements != 0) {
   306	            checkCuda(cudaMemcpy(localC.data(), deviceC,
   307	                                 localElements * sizeof(double),
   308	                                 cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost),
   309	                      "result download", rank);
   310	        }
   311	        std::vector<int> receiveCounts;
   312	        std::vector<int> displacements;
   313	        if (rank == 0) {
   314	            fullC.resize(n * n);
   315	            receiveCounts.resize(rankCount);
   316	            displacements.resize(rankCount);
   317	            for (int process = 0; process < rankCount; ++process) {
   318	                const size_t processRows =
   319	                    baseRows + (static_cast<size_t>(process) < extraRows);
   320	                const size_t processStart = static_cast<size_t>(process) * baseRows +
   321	                    std::min(static_cast<size_t>(process), extraRows);
   322	                if (processRows * n > static_cast<size_t>(INT_MAX) ||
   323	                    processStart * n > static_cast<size_t>(INT_MAX)) {
   324	                    abortWithMessage(rank,
   325	                        "Result is too large for this MPI implementation's Gatherv");
   326	                }
   327	                receiveCounts[process] = static_cast<int>(processRows * n);
   328	                displacements[process] = static_cast<int>(processStart * n);
   329	            }
   330	        }
   331	        if (localElements > static_cast<size_t>(INT_MAX)) {
   332	            abortWithMessage(rank,
   333	                "Local result is too large for this MPI implementation's Gatherv");
   334	        }
   335	        MPI_Gatherv(localC.data(), static_cast<int>(localElements), MPI_DOUBLE,
   336	                    rank == 0 ? fullC.data() : nullptr,
   337	                    rank == 0 ? receiveCounts.data() : nullptr,
   338	                    rank == 0 ? displacements.data() : nullptr, MPI_DOUBLE, 0,
   339	                    MPI_COMM_WORLD);
   340	    }
   341	
   342	    checkCuda(cudaFree(deviceC), "C release", rank);
   343	    checkCuda(cudaFree(deviceA), "A release", rank);
   344	    checkCuda(cudaFree(deviceB), "B release", rank);
   345	    MPI_Comm_free(&localCommunicator);
   346	
   347	    int returnCode = EXIT_SUCCESS;
   348	    if (rank == 0) {
   349	        const long long milliseconds =
   350	            static_cast<long long>(elapsedSeconds * 1000.0);
   351	        std::printf("Computation time: %lld ms\n", milliseconds);
   352	        const double operations = 2.0 * static_cast<double>(n) *
   353	                                  static_cast<double>(n) *
   354	                                  static_cast<double>(n);
   355	        const double gflops = elapsedSeconds > 0.0
   356	                                  ? operations / elapsedSeconds / 1.0e9
   357	                                  : std::numeric_limits<double>::infinity();
   358	        std::printf("Performance: %.3f GFLOPS\n", gflops);
   359	
   360	        if (printResults) {

+void checkCuda(const cudaError_t status, const char* operation, const int rank) {
+    if (status != cudaSuccess) {
+        std::fprintf(stderr, "Rank %d: CUDA error during %s: %s\n", rank,
+                     operation, cudaGetErrorString(status));
+        MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, EXIT_FAILURE);

+void initMatrixRows(double* matrix, const size_t n, const size_t firstRow,
+                    const size_t rows) {
+#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)
+    for (long long localRow = 0; localRow < static_cast<long long>(rows);
+         ++localRow) {
+        const size_t globalRow = firstRow + static_cast<size_t>(localRow);
+        for (size_t column = 0; column < n; ++column) {
+            matrix[static_cast<size_t>(localRow) * n + column] =
+                getPseudoRndValue(n, globalRow, column);
         }
     }

-void matrixMultiply(const std::vector<double>& A, const std::vector<double>& B, 
-                    std::vector<double>& C, const size_t N) {
-    for (size_t i = 0; i < N; ++i) {
-        for (size_t j = 0; j < N; ++j) {
-            double sum = 0.0;
-            for (size_t k = 0; k < N; ++k) {
-                sum += A[i * N + k] * B[k * N + j];
+void initMatrix(double* matrix, const size_t n) {
+    initMatrixRows(matrix, n, 0, n);
+}
+
+// A 32x32 output tile is computed by 256 threads. Each thread accumulates four
+// output rows, increasing arithmetic intensity while retaining coalesced loads.
+__global__ void matrixMultiplyKernel(const double* __restrict__ a,
+                                     const double* __restrict__ b,
+                                     double* __restrict__ c, const int n,
+                                     const int localRows) {
+    __shared__ double aTile[kTile][kTile + 1];
+    __shared__ double bTile[kTile][kTile + 1];
+
+    const int lane = threadIdx.x;
+    const int threadRow = threadIdx.y;
+    const int column = blockIdx.x * kTile + lane;
+    const int rowBase = blockIdx.y * kTile + threadRow;
+    double sums[kTile / kThreadRows] = {0.0, 0.0, 0.0, 0.0};
+
+    for (int tileBase = 0; tileBase < n; tileBase += kTile) {
+#pragma unroll
+        for (int item = 0; item < kTile / kThreadRows; ++item) {
+            const int row = rowBase + item * kThreadRows;
+            const int tileRow = threadRow + item * kThreadRows;
+            aTile[tileRow][lane] =
+                (row < localRows && tileBase + lane < n)
+                    ? a[static_cast<size_t>(row) * n + tileBase + lane]
+                    : 0.0;
+            bTile[tileRow][lane] =
+                (tileBase + tileRow < n && column < n)
+                    ? b[static_cast<size_t>(tileBase + tileRow) * n + column]
+                    : 0.0;
+        }
+        __syncthreads();
+
+#pragma unroll
+        for (int k = 0; k < kTile; ++k) {
+            const double bValue = bTile[k][lane];
+#pragma unroll
+            for (int item = 0; item < kTile / kThreadRows; ++item) {
+                sums[item] = fma(aTile[threadRow + item * kThreadRows][k],
+                                 bValue, sums[item]);
             }
-            C[i * N + j] = sum;
         }
+        __syncthreads();
     }
+
+    if (column < n) {
+#pragma unroll
+        for (int item = 0; item < kTile / kThreadRows; ++item) {
+            const int row = rowBase + item * kThreadRows;
+            if (row < localRows) {
+                c[static_cast<size_t>(row) * n + column] = sums[item];
+            }
+        }
+    }
 }

+                      "result download", rank);
+        }
+        std::vector<int> receiveCounts;
+        std::vector<int> displacements;
+        if (rank == 0) {
+            fullC.resize(n * n);
+            receiveCounts.resize(rankCount);
+            displacements.resize(rankCount);
+            for (int process = 0; process < rankCount; ++process) {
+                const size_t processRows =
+                    baseRows + (static_cast<size_t>(process) < extraRows);
+                const size_t processStart = static_cast<size_t>(process) * baseRows +
+                    std::min(static_cast<size_t>(process), extraRows);
+                if (processRows * n > static_cast<size_t>(INT_MAX) ||
+                    processStart * n > static_cast<size_t>(INT_MAX)) {
+                    abortWithMessage(rank,
+                        "Result is too large for this MPI implementation's Gatherv");
+                }
+                receiveCounts[process] = static_cast<int>(processRows * n);
+                displacements[process] = static_cast<int>(processStart * n);
+            }
+        }
+        if (localElements > static_cast<size_t>(INT_MAX)) {
+            abortWithMessage(rank,
+                "Local result is too large for this MPI implementation's Gatherv");
+        }
+        MPI_Gatherv(localC.data(), static_cast<int>(localElements), MPI_DOUBLE,
+                    rank == 0 ? fullC.data() : nullptr,
+                    rank == 0 ? receiveCounts.data() : nullptr,
+                    rank == 0 ? displacements.data() : nullptr, MPI_DOUBLE, 0,
+                    MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+    }
+
+    checkCuda(cudaFree(deviceC), "C release", rank);
+    checkCuda(cudaFree(deviceA), "A release", rank);
+    checkCuda(cudaFree(deviceB), "B release", rank);
+    MPI_Comm_free(&localCommunicator);
+
+    int returnCode = EXIT_SUCCESS;
+    if (rank == 0) {
+        const long long milliseconds =
+            static_cast<long long>(elapsedSeconds * 1000.0);
+        std::printf("Computation time: %lld ms\n", milliseconds);
+        const double operations = 2.0 * static_cast<double>(n) *
+                                  static_cast<double>(n) *
+                                  static_cast<double>(n);
+        const double gflops = elapsedSeconds > 0.0
+                                  ? operations / elapsedSeconds / 1.0e9
+                                  : std::numeric_limits<double>::infinity();
+        std::printf("Performance: %.3f GFLOPS\n", gflops);
+
+        if (printResults) {
+            print_results(fullC, "MatrixC");
         }
+        if (validate) {
+            std::printf("Validating result...\n");
+            if (validateResult(fullC, n)) {
+                std::printf("Validation: PASSED\n");
+            } else {
+                std::printf("Validation: FAILED\n");
+                returnCode = EXIT_FAILURE;
+            }
+        }
     }
-    
-    return 0;
+
+    MPI_Bcast(&returnCode, 1, MPI_INT, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+    MPI_Finalize();
+    return returnCode;
 }

+void initMatrixRows(double* matrix, const size_t n, const size_t firstRow,
+                    const size_t rows) {
+#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)
+    for (long long localRow = 0; localRow < static_cast<long long>(rows);
+         ++localRow) {
+        const size_t globalRow = firstRow + static_cast<size_t>(localRow);
+        for (size_t column = 0; column < n; ++column) {

-void matrixMultiply(const std::vector<double>& A, const std::vector<double>& B, 
-                    std::vector<double>& C, const size_t N) {

+void initMatrix(double* matrix, const size_t n) {
+    initMatrixRows(matrix, n, 0, n);
+}
+
+// A 32x32 output tile is computed by 256 threads. Each thread accumulates four
+// output rows, increasing arithmetic intensity while retaining coalesced loads.
+__global__ void matrixMultiplyKernel(const double* __restrict__ a,
+                                     const double* __restrict__ b,
+                                     double* __restrict__ c, const int n,
+                                     const int localRows) {
+    __shared__ double aTile[kTile][kTile + 1];
+    __shared__ double bTile[kTile][kTile + 1];
+
+    const int lane = threadIdx.x;
+    const int threadRow = threadIdx.y;
+    const int column = blockIdx.x * kTile + lane;
+    const int rowBase = blockIdx.y * kTile + threadRow;
+    double sums[kTile / kThreadRows] = {0.0, 0.0, 0.0, 0.0};
+
+    for (int tileBase = 0; tileBase < n; tileBase += kTile) {
+#pragma unroll
+        for (int item = 0; item < kTile / kThreadRows; ++item) {
+            const int row = rowBase + item * kThreadRows;
+            const int tileRow = threadRow + item * kThreadRows;
+            aTile[tileRow][lane] =
+                (row < localRows && tileBase + lane < n)
+                    ? a[static_cast<size_t>(row) * n + tileBase + lane]
+                    : 0.0;
+            bTile[tileRow][lane] =
+                (tileBase + tileRow < n && column < n)
+                    ? b[static_cast<size_t>(tileBase + tileRow) * n + column]
+                    : 0.0;
+        }
+        __syncthreads();
+
+#pragma unroll
+        for (int k = 0; k < kTile; ++k) {
+            const double bValue = bTile[k][lane];
+#pragma unroll
+            for (int item = 0; item < kTile / kThreadRows; ++item) {
+                sums[item] = fma(aTile[threadRow + item * kThreadRows][k],
+                                 bValue, sums[item]);
             }
-            C[i * N + j] = sum;
         }
+        __syncthreads();
     }
+
+    if (column < n) {
+#pragma unroll
+        for (int item = 0; item < kTile / kThreadRows; ++item) {
+            const int row = rowBase + item * kThreadRows;
+            if (row < localRows) {
+                c[static_cast<size_t>(row) * n + column] = sums[item];
+            }
+        }
+    }
 }

codex
The multi-rank boundary tests pass, including a non-tile-aligned 513×513 case and a case with more MPI ranks than matrix rows. One performance refinement is warranted: the custom tiled kernel is correct, but NVIDIA’s tuned DGEMM implementation is substantially better across differing accelerator generations. I’m switching the device-local multiply to CUDA’s bundled cuBLAS while preserving the MPI row decomposition and OpenMP work; this adds no dependency outside the already-required CUDA toolkit.

+namespace {
+
+// Generate pseudo-random values for matrix initialization.
+constexpr double getPseudoRndValue(const size_t n, const size_t i,
+                                   const size_t j) noexcept {
+    return (((i + 1) * (i + j + 1) * 1299709) % (n * n)) /
+           static_cast<double>(n * n);
+}
+
+void abortWithMessage(const int rank, const char* message) {
+    if (rank == 0) {
+        std::fprintf(stderr, "%s\n", message);
+    }
+    MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, EXIT_FAILURE);
+}
+
+void checkCuda(const cudaError_t status, const char* operation, const int rank) {
+    if (status != cudaSuccess) {
+        std::fprintf(stderr, "Rank %d: CUDA error during %s: %s\n", rank,
+                     operation, cudaGetErrorString(status));
+        MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, EXIT_FAILURE);
+    }
 }

+void checkCublas(const cublasStatus_t status, const char* operation,
+                 const int rank) {
+    if (status != CUBLAS_STATUS_SUCCESS) {
+        std::fprintf(stderr, "Rank %d: cuBLAS error during %s (status %d)\n",
+                     rank, operation, static_cast<int>(status));
+        MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, EXIT_FAILURE);

+void initMatrixRows(double* matrix, const size_t n, const size_t firstRow,
+                    const size_t rows) {
+#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)
+    for (long long localRow = 0; localRow < static_cast<long long>(rows);
+         ++localRow) {
+        const size_t globalRow = firstRow + static_cast<size_t>(localRow);
+        for (size_t column = 0; column < n; ++column) {
+            matrix[static_cast<size_t>(localRow) * n + column] =
+                getPseudoRndValue(n, globalRow, column);
         }
     }

+                    processStart * n > static_cast<size_t>(INT_MAX)) {

+            abortWithMessage(rank,
+                "Local result is too large for this MPI implementation's Gatherv");
+        }

+                    rank == 0 ? fullC.data() : nullptr,
+                    rank == 0 ? receiveCounts.data() : nullptr,
+                    rank == 0 ? displacements.data() : nullptr, MPI_DOUBLE, 0,

-            return 1;

+void initMatrixRows(double* matrix, const size_t n, const size_t firstRow,
+                    const size_t rows) {
+#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)
+    for (long long localRow = 0; localRow < static_cast<long long>(rows);
+         ++localRow) {
+        const size_t globalRow = firstRow + static_cast<size_t>(localRow);

+// Generate pseudo-random values for matrix initialization.
+constexpr double getPseudoRndValue(const size_t n, const size_t i,

+void initMatrixRows(double* matrix, const size_t n, const size_t firstRow,
+                    const size_t rows) {
+#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)
+    for (long long localRow = 0; localRow < static_cast<long long>(rows);
+         ++localRow) {
+        const size_t globalRow = firstRow + static_cast<size_t>(localRow);
+        for (size_t column = 0; column < n; ++column) {
