   162	            }
   163	            argumentsValid = false;
   164	        }
   165	    }
   166	
   167	    if (showHelp || !argumentsValid) {
   168	        if (rank == 0) {
   169	            printUsage(argv[0]);
   170	        }
   171	        MPI_Finalize();
   172	        return argumentsValid ? EXIT_SUCCESS : EXIT_FAILURE;
   173	    }
   174	    if (n > std::numeric_limits<size_t>::max() / n ||
   175	        n * n > std::numeric_limits<size_t>::max() / sizeof(double)) {
   176	        abortWithMessage(rank, "Matrix dimensions overflow addressable memory");
   177	    }
   178	
   179	    // Contiguous block-row distribution, balanced to within one row.
   180	    const size_t baseRows = n / static_cast<size_t>(rankCount);
   181	    const size_t extraRows = n % static_cast<size_t>(rankCount);
   182	    const size_t localRows = baseRows + (static_cast<size_t>(rank) < extraRows);
   183	    const size_t firstRow = static_cast<size_t>(rank) * baseRows +
   184	                            std::min(static_cast<size_t>(rank), extraRows);
   185	    const size_t localElements = localRows * n;
   186	
   187	    MPI_Comm localCommunicator = MPI_COMM_NULL;
   188	    MPI_Comm_split_type(MPI_COMM_WORLD, MPI_COMM_TYPE_SHARED, rank,
   189	                        MPI_INFO_NULL, &localCommunicator);
   190	    int localRank = 0;
   191	    MPI_Comm_rank(localCommunicator, &localRank);
   192	    int deviceCount = 0;
   193	    checkCuda(cudaGetDeviceCount(&deviceCount), "device discovery", rank);
   194	    if (deviceCount == 0) {
   195	        abortWithMessage(rank, "No CUDA device is available");
   196	    }
   197	    checkCuda(cudaSetDevice(localRank % deviceCount), "device selection", rank);
   198	    cublasHandle_t blasHandle = nullptr;
   199	    checkCublas(cublasCreate(&blasHandle), "handle creation", rank);
   200	    checkCublas(cublasSetMathMode(blasHandle, CUBLAS_DEFAULT_MATH),
   201	                "math mode selection", rank);
   202	
   203	    if (rank == 0) {
   204	        std::printf("Matrix Multiplication Benchmark\n");
   205	        std::printf("Matrix size: %zu x %zu\n", n, n);
   206	        std::printf("MPI ranks: %d, OpenMP threads/rank: %d\n", rankCount,
   207	                    omp_get_max_threads());
   208	        std::printf("Validation: %s\n", validate ? "enabled" : "disabled");
   209	        std::printf("Initializing matrices...\n");
   210	    }
   211	
   212	    std::vector<double> localA(localElements);
   213	    std::vector<double> b(n * n);
   214	    initMatrixRows(localA.data(), n, firstRow, localRows);
   215	    initMatrix(b.data(), n);
   216	
   217	    double* deviceA = nullptr;
   218	    double* deviceB = nullptr;
   219	    double* deviceC = nullptr;
   220	    checkCuda(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&deviceB), n * n * sizeof(double)),
   221	              "B allocation", rank);
   222	    checkCuda(cudaMemcpy(deviceB, b.data(), n * n * sizeof(double),
   223	                         cudaMemcpyHostToDevice),
   224	              "B upload", rank);
   225	    if (localElements != 0) {
   226	        checkCuda(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&deviceA),
   227	                             localElements * sizeof(double)),
   228	                  "A allocation", rank);
   229	        checkCuda(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&deviceC),
   230	                             localElements * sizeof(double)),
   231	                  "C allocation", rank);
   232	        checkCuda(cudaMemcpy(deviceA, localA.data(), localElements * sizeof(double),
   233	                             cudaMemcpyHostToDevice),
   234	                  "A upload", rank);
   235	    }
   236	
   237	    const auto multiplyLocal = [&]() {
   238	        const double alpha = 1.0;
   239	        const double beta = 0.0;
   240	        // Row-major C=A*B is column-major C^T=B^T*A^T in the same buffers.
   241	        checkCublas(cublasDgemm(blasHandle, CUBLAS_OP_N, CUBLAS_OP_N,
   242	                                static_cast<int>(n),
   243	                                static_cast<int>(localRows),
   244	                                static_cast<int>(n), &alpha, deviceB,
   245	                                static_cast<int>(n), deviceA,
   246	                                static_cast<int>(n), &beta, deviceC,
   247	                                static_cast<int>(n)),
   248	                    "matrix multiplication", rank);
   249	    };
   250	
   251	    // Force lazy CUDA/cuBLAS module setup out of the measured steady-state
   252	    // operation. The timed call below overwrites this warm-up result.
   253	    if (localRows != 0) {
   254	        multiplyLocal();
   255	        checkCuda(cudaDeviceSynchronize(), "matrix multiplication warm-up", rank);
   256	    }
   257	
   258	    if (rank == 0) {
   259	        std::printf("Computing matrix multiplication...\n");
   260	    }
   261	    MPI_Barrier(MPI_COMM_WORLD);
   262	    const double start = MPI_Wtime();
   263	    if (localRows != 0) {
   264	        multiplyLocal();
   265	        checkCuda(cudaDeviceSynchronize(), "matrix multiplication", rank);
   266	    }
   267	    const double localSeconds = MPI_Wtime() - start;
   268	    double elapsedSeconds = 0.0;
   269	    MPI_Reduce(&localSeconds, &elapsedSeconds, 1, MPI_DOUBLE, MPI_MAX, 0,
   270	               MPI_COMM_WORLD);
   271	
   272	    std::vector<double> fullC;
   273	    if (validate || printResults) {
   274	        std::vector<double> localC(localElements);
   275	        if (localElements != 0) {
   276	            checkCuda(cudaMemcpy(localC.data(), deviceC,
   277	                                 localElements * sizeof(double),
   278	                                 cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost),
   279	                      "result download", rank);
   280	        }
   281	        std::vector<int> receiveCounts;
   282	        std::vector<int> displacements;
   283	        if (rank == 0) {
   284	            fullC.resize(n * n);
   285	            receiveCounts.resize(rankCount);
   286	            displacements.resize(rankCount);
   287	            for (int process = 0; process < rankCount; ++process) {
   288	                const size_t processRows =
   289	                    baseRows + (static_cast<size_t>(process) < extraRows);
   290	                const size_t processStart = static_cast<size_t>(process) * baseRows +
   291	                    std::min(static_cast<size_t>(process), extraRows);
   292	                if (processRows * n > static_cast<size_t>(INT_MAX) ||
   293	                    processStart * n > static_cast<size_t>(INT_MAX)) {
   294	                    abortWithMessage(rank,
   295	                        "Result is too large for this MPI implementation's Gatherv");
   296	                }
   297	                receiveCounts[process] = static_cast<int>(processRows * n);
   298	                displacements[process] = static_cast<int>(processStart * n);
   299	            }
   300	        }
   301	        if (localElements > static_cast<size_t>(INT_MAX)) {
   302	            abortWithMessage(rank,
   303	                "Local result is too large for this MPI implementation's Gatherv");
   304	        }
   305	        MPI_Gatherv(localC.data(), static_cast<int>(localElements), MPI_DOUBLE,
   306	                    rank == 0 ? fullC.data() : nullptr,
   307	                    rank == 0 ? receiveCounts.data() : nullptr,
   308	                    rank == 0 ? displacements.data() : nullptr, MPI_DOUBLE, 0,
   309	                    MPI_COMM_WORLD);
   310	    }
   311	
   312	    checkCuda(cudaFree(deviceC), "C release", rank);
   313	    checkCuda(cudaFree(deviceA), "A release", rank);
   314	    checkCuda(cudaFree(deviceB), "B release", rank);
   315	    checkCublas(cublasDestroy(blasHandle), "handle destruction", rank);
   316	    MPI_Comm_free(&localCommunicator);
   317	
   318	    int returnCode = EXIT_SUCCESS;
   319	    if (rank == 0) {
   320	        const long long milliseconds =
   321	            static_cast<long long>(elapsedSeconds * 1000.0);
   322	        std::printf("Computation time: %lld ms\n", milliseconds);
   323	        const double operations = 2.0 * static_cast<double>(n) *
   324	                                  static_cast<double>(n) *
   325	                                  static_cast<double>(n);
   326	        const double gflops = elapsedSeconds > 0.0
   327	                                  ? operations / elapsedSeconds / 1.0e9
   328	                                  : std::numeric_limits<double>::infinity();
   329	        std::printf("Performance: %.3f GFLOPS\n", gflops);
   330	
   331	        if (printResults) {
   332	            print_results(fullC, "MatrixC");
   333	        }
   334	        if (validate) {
   335	            std::printf("Validating result...\n");
   336	            if (validateResult(fullC, n)) {
   337	                std::printf("Validation: PASSED\n");
   338	            } else {
   339	                std::printf("Validation: FAILED\n");
   340	                returnCode = EXIT_FAILURE;
     1	cmake_minimum_required(VERSION 3.24)
     2	
     3	# Build for the accelerator present on the target cluster unless explicitly
     4	# overridden (for example with -DCMAKE_CUDA_ARCHITECTURES=80;90).
     5	if(NOT DEFINED CMAKE_CUDA_ARCHITECTURES)
     6	    set(CMAKE_CUDA_ARCHITECTURES native)
     7	endif()

+                 const int rank) {

+void initMatrixRows(double* matrix, const size_t n, const size_t firstRow,
+                    const size_t rows) {
+#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)
+    for (long long localRow = 0; localRow < static_cast<long long>(rows);
+         ++localRow) {
+        const size_t globalRow = firstRow + static_cast<size_t>(localRow);
+        for (size_t column = 0; column < n; ++column) {
+            matrix[static_cast<size_t>(localRow) * n + column] =
+                getPseudoRndValue(n, globalRow, column);
         }
     }

+                            std::min(static_cast<size_t>(rank), extraRows);
+    const size_t localElements = localRows * n;
+
+    MPI_Comm localCommunicator = MPI_COMM_NULL;
+    MPI_Comm_split_type(MPI_COMM_WORLD, MPI_COMM_TYPE_SHARED, rank,
+                        MPI_INFO_NULL, &localCommunicator);
+    int localRank = 0;
+    MPI_Comm_rank(localCommunicator, &localRank);
+    int deviceCount = 0;
+    checkCuda(cudaGetDeviceCount(&deviceCount), "device discovery", rank);
+    if (deviceCount == 0) {
+        abortWithMessage(rank, "No CUDA device is available");
+    }
+    checkCuda(cudaSetDevice(localRank % deviceCount), "device selection", rank);
+    cublasHandle_t blasHandle = nullptr;
+    checkCublas(cublasCreate(&blasHandle), "handle creation", rank);
+    checkCublas(cublasSetMathMode(blasHandle, CUBLAS_DEFAULT_MATH),
+                "math mode selection", rank);
+
+    if (rank == 0) {
+        std::printf("Matrix Multiplication Benchmark\n");
+        std::printf("Matrix size: %zu x %zu\n", n, n);
+        std::printf("MPI ranks: %d, OpenMP threads/rank: %d\n", rankCount,
+                    omp_get_max_threads());
+        std::printf("Validation: %s\n", validate ? "enabled" : "disabled");
+        std::printf("Initializing matrices...\n");
+    }
+
+    std::vector<double> localA(localElements);
+    std::vector<double> b(n * n);
+    initMatrixRows(localA.data(), n, firstRow, localRows);
+    initMatrix(b.data(), n);
+
+    double* deviceA = nullptr;
+    double* deviceB = nullptr;
+    double* deviceC = nullptr;
+    checkCuda(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&deviceB), n * n * sizeof(double)),
+              "B allocation", rank);
+    checkCuda(cudaMemcpy(deviceB, b.data(), n * n * sizeof(double),
+                         cudaMemcpyHostToDevice),
+              "B upload", rank);
+    if (localElements != 0) {
+        checkCuda(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&deviceA),
+                             localElements * sizeof(double)),
+                  "A allocation", rank);
+        checkCuda(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&deviceC),
+                             localElements * sizeof(double)),
+                  "C allocation", rank);
+        checkCuda(cudaMemcpy(deviceA, localA.data(), localElements * sizeof(double),
+                             cudaMemcpyHostToDevice),
+                  "A upload", rank);
+    }
+
+    const auto multiplyLocal = [&]() {
+        const double alpha = 1.0;
+        const double beta = 0.0;
+        // Row-major C=A*B is column-major C^T=B^T*A^T in the same buffers.
+        checkCublas(cublasDgemm(blasHandle, CUBLAS_OP_N, CUBLAS_OP_N,
+                                static_cast<int>(n),
+                                static_cast<int>(localRows),
+                                static_cast<int>(n), &alpha, deviceB,
+                                static_cast<int>(n), deviceA,
+                                static_cast<int>(n), &beta, deviceC,
+                                static_cast<int>(n)),
+                    "matrix multiplication", rank);
+    };
+
+    // Force lazy CUDA/cuBLAS module setup out of the measured steady-state
+    // operation. The timed call below overwrites this warm-up result.
+    if (localRows != 0) {
+        multiplyLocal();
+        checkCuda(cudaDeviceSynchronize(), "matrix multiplication warm-up", rank);
+    }
+
+    if (rank == 0) {
+        std::printf("Computing matrix multiplication...\n");
+    }
+    MPI_Barrier(MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+    const double start = MPI_Wtime();
+    if (localRows != 0) {
+        multiplyLocal();
+        checkCuda(cudaDeviceSynchronize(), "matrix multiplication", rank);
+    }
+    const double localSeconds = MPI_Wtime() - start;
+    double elapsedSeconds = 0.0;
+    MPI_Reduce(&localSeconds, &elapsedSeconds, 1, MPI_DOUBLE, MPI_MAX, 0,
+               MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+
+    std::vector<double> fullC;
+    if (validate || printResults) {
+        std::vector<double> localC(localElements);
+        if (localElements != 0) {
+            checkCuda(cudaMemcpy(localC.data(), deviceC,
+                                 localElements * sizeof(double),
+                                 cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost),
+                      "result download", rank);
+        }
+        std::vector<int> receiveCounts;
+        std::vector<int> displacements;
+        if (rank == 0) {
+            fullC.resize(n * n);
+            receiveCounts.resize(rankCount);
+            displacements.resize(rankCount);
+            for (int process = 0; process < rankCount; ++process) {
+                const size_t processRows =
+                    baseRows + (static_cast<size_t>(process) < extraRows);
+                const size_t processStart = static_cast<size_t>(process) * baseRows +
+                    std::min(static_cast<size_t>(process), extraRows);
+                if (processRows * n > static_cast<size_t>(INT_MAX) ||
+                    processStart * n > static_cast<size_t>(INT_MAX)) {
+                    abortWithMessage(rank,
+                        "Result is too large for this MPI implementation's Gatherv");
+                }
+                receiveCounts[process] = static_cast<int>(processRows * n);
+                displacements[process] = static_cast<int>(processStart * n);
+            }
+        }
+        if (localElements > static_cast<size_t>(INT_MAX)) {
+            abortWithMessage(rank,
+                "Local result is too large for this MPI implementation's Gatherv");
         }
+        MPI_Gatherv(localC.data(), static_cast<int>(localElements), MPI_DOUBLE,
+                    rank == 0 ? fullC.data() : nullptr,
+                    rank == 0 ? receiveCounts.data() : nullptr,
+                    rank == 0 ? displacements.data() : nullptr, MPI_DOUBLE, 0,
+                    MPI_COMM_WORLD);
     }
-    
-    return 0;
+
+    checkCuda(cudaFree(deviceC), "C release", rank);
+    checkCuda(cudaFree(deviceA), "A release", rank);
+    checkCuda(cudaFree(deviceB), "B release", rank);
+    checkCublas(cublasDestroy(blasHandle), "handle destruction", rank);
+    MPI_Comm_free(&localCommunicator);
+
+    int returnCode = EXIT_SUCCESS;
+    if (rank == 0) {
+        const long long milliseconds =
+            static_cast<long long>(elapsedSeconds * 1000.0);
+        std::printf("Computation time: %lld ms\n", milliseconds);
+        const double operations = 2.0 * static_cast<double>(n) *
+                                  static_cast<double>(n) *
+                                  static_cast<double>(n);
+        const double gflops = elapsedSeconds > 0.0
+                                  ? operations / elapsedSeconds / 1.0e9
+                                  : std::numeric_limits<double>::infinity();
+        std::printf("Performance: %.3f GFLOPS\n", gflops);
+
+        if (printResults) {
+            print_results(fullC, "MatrixC");
+        }
+        if (validate) {
+            std::printf("Validating result...\n");
+            if (validateResult(fullC, n)) {
+                std::printf("Validation: PASSED\n");
+            } else {
+                std::printf("Validation: FAILED\n");
+                returnCode = EXIT_FAILURE;
+            }
+        }
+    }
+
+    MPI_Bcast(&returnCode, 1, MPI_INT, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+    MPI_Finalize();
+    return returnCode;
 }

codex
Implemented the unconditional hybrid MPI + OpenMP + CUDA benchmark.

- MPI distributes matrix rows across ranks and maps local ranks to GPUs.
- OpenMP parallelizes initialization and validation.
- cuBLAS DGEMM provides optimized GPU multiplication.
- Timing uses the slowest MPI rank for accurate aggregate GFLOPS.
- Results are gathered only when validation or result printing is requested.
- CMake now requires MPI, OpenMP, and CUDA, retaining executable name `matmul`.
- Native GPU architecture is selected by default.

Changed:

- [matmul.cpp](/home/llmtest/evals/20260805-120633/matmul_gpt-5.6-sol-medium_hybrid_r1/matmul/matmul.cpp:125)
- [CMakeLists.txt](/home/llmtest/evals/20260805-120633/matmul_gpt-5.6-sol-medium_hybrid_r1/matmul/CMakeLists.txt:1)

Verified with CUDA 12, OpenMPI, and OpenMP. Multi-rank tests passed validation for aligned, non-aligned, and more-ranks-than-rows cases, including `513×513` across two MPI ranks.

+void initMatrixRows(double* matrix, const size_t n, const size_t firstRow,
+                    const size_t rows) {
+#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)
+    for (long long localRow = 0; localRow < static_cast<long long>(rows);
+         ++localRow) {
+        const size_t globalRow = firstRow + static_cast<size_t>(localRow);
+        for (size_t column = 0; column < n; ++column) {
+            matrix[static_cast<size_t>(localRow) * n + column] =
+                getPseudoRndValue(n, globalRow, column);
         }
     }

+
+    const auto multiplyLocal = [&]() {
+        const double alpha = 1.0;
+        const double beta = 0.0;
+        // Row-major C=A*B is column-major C^T=B^T*A^T in the same buffers.
+        checkCublas(cublasDgemm(blasHandle, CUBLAS_OP_N, CUBLAS_OP_N,
+                                static_cast<int>(n),
+                                static_cast<int>(localRows),
+                                static_cast<int>(n), &alpha, deviceB,
+                                static_cast<int>(n), deviceA,
+                                static_cast<int>(n), &beta, deviceC,
+                                static_cast<int>(n)),
+                    "matrix multiplication", rank);
+    };
+
+    // Force lazy CUDA/cuBLAS module setup out of the measured steady-state
+    // operation. The timed call below overwrites this warm-up result.
+    if (localRows != 0) {
+        multiplyLocal();
+        checkCuda(cudaDeviceSynchronize(), "matrix multiplication warm-up", rank);
+    }
+
+    if (rank == 0) {
+        std::printf("Computing matrix multiplication...\n");
+    }
+    MPI_Barrier(MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+    const double start = MPI_Wtime();
+    if (localRows != 0) {
+        multiplyLocal();
+        checkCuda(cudaDeviceSynchronize(), "matrix multiplication", rank);
+    }
+    const double localSeconds = MPI_Wtime() - start;
+    double elapsedSeconds = 0.0;
+    MPI_Reduce(&localSeconds, &elapsedSeconds, 1, MPI_DOUBLE, MPI_MAX, 0,
+               MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+
+    std::vector<double> fullC;
+    if (validate || printResults) {
+        std::vector<double> localC(localElements);
+        if (localElements != 0) {
+            checkCuda(cudaMemcpy(localC.data(), deviceC,
+                                 localElements * sizeof(double),
+                                 cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost),
+                      "result download", rank);
+        }
+        std::vector<int> receiveCounts;
+        std::vector<int> displacements;
+        if (rank == 0) {
+            fullC.resize(n * n);
+            receiveCounts.resize(rankCount);
+            displacements.resize(rankCount);
+            for (int process = 0; process < rankCount; ++process) {
+                const size_t processRows =
+                    baseRows + (static_cast<size_t>(process) < extraRows);
+                const size_t processStart = static_cast<size_t>(process) * baseRows +
+                    std::min(static_cast<size_t>(process), extraRows);
+                if (processRows * n > static_cast<size_t>(INT_MAX) ||
+                    processStart * n > static_cast<size_t>(INT_MAX)) {
+                    abortWithMessage(rank,
+                        "Result is too large for this MPI implementation's Gatherv");
+                }
+                receiveCounts[process] = static_cast<int>(processRows * n);
+                displacements[process] = static_cast<int>(processStart * n);
+            }
+        }
+        if (localElements > static_cast<size_t>(INT_MAX)) {
+            abortWithMessage(rank,
+                "Local result is too large for this MPI implementation's Gatherv");
         }
+        MPI_Gatherv(localC.data(), static_cast<int>(localElements), MPI_DOUBLE,
+                    rank == 0 ? fullC.data() : nullptr,
+                    rank == 0 ? receiveCounts.data() : nullptr,
+                    rank == 0 ? displacements.data() : nullptr, MPI_DOUBLE, 0,
+                    MPI_COMM_WORLD);
     }
-    
-    return 0;
+
+    checkCuda(cudaFree(deviceC), "C release", rank);
+    checkCuda(cudaFree(deviceA), "A release", rank);
+    checkCuda(cudaFree(deviceB), "B release", rank);
+    checkCublas(cublasDestroy(blasHandle), "handle destruction", rank);
+    MPI_Comm_free(&localCommunicator);
+
+    int returnCode = EXIT_SUCCESS;
+    if (rank == 0) {
+        const long long milliseconds =
+            static_cast<long long>(elapsedSeconds * 1000.0);
+        std::printf("Computation time: %lld ms\n", milliseconds);
+        const double operations = 2.0 * static_cast<double>(n) *
+                                  static_cast<double>(n) *
+                                  static_cast<double>(n);
+        const double gflops = elapsedSeconds > 0.0
+                                  ? operations / elapsedSeconds / 1.0e9
+                                  : std::numeric_limits<double>::infinity();
+        std::printf("Performance: %.3f GFLOPS\n", gflops);
+
+        if (printResults) {
+            print_results(fullC, "MatrixC");
+        }
+        if (validate) {
+            std::printf("Validating result...\n");
+            if (validateResult(fullC, n)) {
+                std::printf("Validation: PASSED\n");
+            } else {
+                std::printf("Validation: FAILED\n");
+                returnCode = EXIT_FAILURE;
+            }
+        }
+    }
+
+    MPI_Bcast(&returnCode, 1, MPI_INT, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+    MPI_Finalize();
+    return returnCode;
 }

tokens used
75,189
Implemented the unconditional hybrid MPI + OpenMP + CUDA benchmark.

- MPI distributes matrix rows across ranks and maps local ranks to GPUs.
- OpenMP parallelizes initialization and validation.
- cuBLAS DGEMM provides optimized GPU multiplication.
- Timing uses the slowest MPI rank for accurate aggregate GFLOPS.
- Results are gathered only when validation or result printing is requested.
- CMake now requires MPI, OpenMP, and CUDA, retaining executable name `matmul`.
- Native GPU architecture is selected by default.

Verified with CUDA 12, OpenMPI, and OpenMP. Multi-rank tests passed validation for aligned, non-aligned, and more-ranks-than-rows cases, including `513×513` across two MPI ranks.
